import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class DataService {

  private apiUrl = 'https://example.com/api/data';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) { }

  getData(): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get(this.apiUrl);
  }

  getDataByParam(param: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get(`${this.apiUrl}?${param}`);
  }

  postData(data: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(this.apiUrl, data);
  }

  putData(data: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.put(`${this.apiUrl}?${data}`);
  }

  deleteData(): Observable<any>

9: PPL-XL=1.304, , score=0.265

The FDA has approved a new drug to treat a rare genetic disorder called Fabry disease. The drug, called Migalastat, is the first of its kind to be approved for this condition.
Fabry disease is a rare genetic disorder that affects the body's ability to produce an enzyme called alpha-galactosidase A. This enzyme is necessary for the breakdown and removal of a fatty substance called globotriaosylsphingosine (Lewandowsky et al., 2017). Without enough alpha-galactosidase A, globotriaosylsphingosine accumulates in the body and can cause damage to the kidneys, heart, and other organs.
Migalastat is a chaperone therapy, which means it helps the body produce more of the missing enzyme. The drug works by binding to the enzyme and helping it to fold into its correct shape, which allows it to function properly.
The FDA approved Migalastat based on the results of a clinical trial that showed it was safe and effective in treating Fab

        delay: 300, // delay in milliseconds between searches
        maxResults: 5 // maximum number of results to display
    };

    function Autocomplete(element, options) {
        this.element = element;
        this.options = $.extend({}, defaults, options);
        this.results = [];
        this.currentValue = '';
        this.debounce = false;
        this.delay = 0;
    }

    Autocomplete.prototype = {
        init: function() {
            var that = this;
            this.element.on('input', function() {
                if (that.debounce) {
                    clearTimeout(that.debounce);
                }
                that.debounce = setTimeout(function() {
                    that.search();
                }, that.options.delay);
            });

The FDA has approved a new drug to treat a rare genetic disorder called Fabry disease. The drug, called Migalastat, is the first of its kind to be approved for this condition.
Fabry disease is a rare genetic disorder that affects the body's ability to produce an enzyme called alpha-galactosidase A. This enzyme is necessary for the breakdown and removal of a fatty substance called globotriaosylsphingosine (Lewandowsky et al., 2017). Without enough alpha-galactosidase A, globotriaosylsphingosine accumulates in the body and can cause damage to the kidneys, heart, and other organs.
Migalastat is a chaperone therapy, which means it helps the body produce more of the missing enzyme. The drug works by binding to the enzyme and helping it to fold into its correct shape, which allows it to function properly.
The FDA approved Migalastat based on the results of a clinical trial that showed it was safe and effective in treating Fab

The FDA has approved a new drug to treat a rare genetic disorder called Fabry disease. The drug, called Migalastat, is the first of its kind to be approved for this condition.
Fabry disease is a rare genetic disorder that affects the body's ability to produce an enzyme called alpha-galactosidase A. This enzyme is necessary for the breakdown and removal of a fatty substance called globotriaosylsphingosine (Lewandowsky et al., 2017). Without enough alpha-galactosidase A, globotriaosylsphingosine accumulates in the body and can cause damage to the kidneys, heart, and other organs.
Migalastat is a chaperone therapy, which means it helps the body produce more of the missing enzyme. The drug works by binding to the enzyme and helping it to fold into its correct shape, which allows it to function properly.
The FDA approved Migalastat based on the results of a clinical trial that showed it was safe and effective in treating Fab

    'use strict';
    var pluginName = 'autocomplete';
    var defaults = {
        source: null, // array of search terms
        minChars: 1, // minimum number of characters to match
        delay: 300, // delay in milliseconds between searches
        maxResults: 5 // maximum number of results to display
    };

    function Autocomplete(element, options) {
        this.element = element;
        this.options = $.extend({}, defaults, options);
        this.results = [];
        this.currentValue = '';
        this.debounce = false;
        this.delay = 0;
    }

    Autocomplete.prototype = {
        init: function() {
            var that = this;
            this.element.on('input', function() {
                if (that.debounce) {
                    clearTimeout(that.debounce);
                }
                that.debounce = setTimeout(function() {
                    that.search();
                }, that.options.delay);
            });
        },

import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class DataService {

  private apiUrl = 'https://example.com/api/data';

  constructor(private http: HttpClient) { }

  getData(): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get(this.apiUrl);
  }

  getDataByParam(param: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.get(`${this.apiUrl}?${param}`);
  }

  postData(data: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.post(this.apiUrl, data);
  }

  putData(data: any): Observable<any> {
    return this.http.put(`${this.apiUrl}?${data}`);
  }

  deleteData(): Observable<any>

Home > Products > Mining Equipment > Sand Washing Machine
Sand washing machine is a kind of equipment used in the sand production line to wash and separate the sand from the impurities. It is also known as sand washer, sand cleaner, or sand scrubber. The machine is designed to remove the impurities such as dust, stone, and other debris from the sand, leaving it clean and ready for use.
Sand washing machine is widely used in various industries such as construction, glass, foundry, and landscaping. It is available in different sizes and capacities to suit the specific needs of different industries.
The working principle of sand washing machine is based on the movement of water and mechanical agitation. The sand is fed into the machine through the feeding hopper, and the water is sprayed on the sand through the spray nozzles, creating a scrubbing action that removes the impurities. The sand and water mixture then passes through the rotating drum, where the impurities are separated and removed. The clean sand is then discharged through the discharge port, while the dirty water is recycled
